# All cases and projects

[Editor picks](README.md) | [中文总目录](INDEX.md)

**Explore software and hardware with AI: rebuild features, bridge protocols, and modernize legacy systems.**

**Tracks public REA and AI reverse-engineering cases six times daily, automatically screening, deduplicating, and updating the collection.**

A collection of REA and AI reverse-engineering cases and projects, covering game preservation, hardware interoperability, app reconstruction, and algorithm research. Each entry explains the target, the author’s purpose and approach, and links to original posts, code, or verification records.

[REA](https://github.com/morluto/rea) (Reverse Engineer Anything) lets coding agents call local reverse-engineering tools through CLI and MCP interfaces. This collection also includes work with other AI tools and interoperability projects built for AI agents.

**1276 entries**, organized by use case, without a Top-N or popularity threshold.

English case details and category pages are available throughout this reading path. Contribution and maintenance guides are in Chinese.

## Contents

- [Games and preservation](categories/en/games.md) (267)
- [Hardware and interoperability](categories/en/hardware.md) (351)
- [Applications and clients](categories/en/apps.md) (531)
- [Algorithms and feature recovery](categories/en/algorithms.md) (127)
- [Contribute a case (Chinese)](CONTRIBUTING.md) · [Maintenance (Chinese)](MAINTENANCE.md)

Tool labels distinguish REA, other AI work, and unconfirmed REA use. Stage labels—Needs verification, In progress, Analysis test, and Related project—keep all entries discoverable without presenting them as completed results.

- [Caleb Gross: using an LLM to prioritize decompiled firmware functions for review](cases/en/caleb-gross-llm-firmware-function-ranking.md) — Black Hat describes Caleb Gross ranking symbol-free firmware functions by likely bugs, reporting more than twice the precision of directly asking a model. Experimental details and independent reproduction are unavailable. **[Other AI-assisted work · Analysis test]**
- [PHP and LiteLLM: a Claude patch-diff, cited-diagram and quiz learning idea](cases/en/php-litellm-claude-patch-learning-idea.md) — The author proposes comparing PHP before and after two security fixes with Claude Code/Opus, citing every diagram node by file and line, then checking understanding with questions. LiteLLM is another proposed target; no execution results are supplied. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**
- [A record.c binary lab: constrain Claude reversing with competing explanations and original-program tests](cases/en/claude-record-binary-evidence-lab-idea.md) — The author proposes a known-answer record.c lab, splitting function mapping, competing specifications and original-binary tests across models. A valid_record 16/32 boundary illustrates why model explanations need testing; the lab was not independently run. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**
- [CREBench: 432 cryptographic binary tasks, from algorithm identification to flag recovery](cases/en/crebench-432-crypto-re-benchmark-report.md) — CREBench combines 48 algorithms, three key modes and three difficulties into 432 challenges for LLM algorithm identification, key extraction, wrapper reimplementation and flag recovery. The official README was read; scores were not rerun. **[Other AI-assisted work · Analysis test]**
- [Claude and Theorem: turning OSS-Fuzz binaries into Rocq no-crash proofs](cases/en/anthropic-theorem-claude-binary-formal-verification.md) — Anthropic and Theorem report Claude-written RISC-V machine-code proofs for 239 entry points across 123 libraries, checked by Rocq. These apply to a specific single-threaded static Linux model and inputs below 64MB, not bug-free entire libraries. **[Other AI-assisted work · Analysis test]**
- [MalwareBench: a cross-platform benchmark from obfuscated malware to behavioral reports and extractors](cases/en/malwarebench-cross-platform-ai-re-benchmark.md) — General Analysis introduces an end-to-end defensive reversing benchmark, reporting GPT-6.1 Sol in the lead at just 4.42%. Its Conti example still fails to decrypt a later payload: partial analysis is not completion. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**
- [Prime-broker positioning screenshots: a reported use of AI to recover time series and assess data value](cases/en/prime-broker-positioning-screenshot-ai-timeseries.md) — The poster says AI can turn prime-broker positioning screenshots into underlying time series to distinguish useful data from Goldman Sachs marketing; no sample, model or reconstruction error is supplied. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**
- [Social-media virality prediction: Alin A's abandoned idea to investigate recommendation algorithms with REA](cases/en/social-media-viral-probability-rea-abandoned-idea.md) — Alin A proposes investigating recommendation algorithms, extracting a virality-probability model and automating publishing for follower growth, but explicitly abandons the idea; no platform, dataset or prediction result is given. **[REA · Needs verification]**
- [Xiaomi exercise calorie accumulation: a report of DeepSeek investigation into repeat counting on refresh](cases/en/xiaomi-motion-deepseek-calorie-refresh-report.md) — LanzhiAI relays a DeepSeek-assisted Xiaomi exercise-algorithm report: repeated refreshes in one minute allegedly turn 40 steps from 1.6 to 8.28 calories; follow-ups still await captures and full reproduction. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**
- [CUDA: a reader's challenge to try decompilation with an agent swarm](cases/en/cuda-agent-swarm-decompile-idea.md) — loveofdoing proposes having an agent swarm decompile CUDA, without identifying a component, version, compatibility goal or attempted implementation. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**
- [LLM4Decompile: specialized models recovering C functions from assembly or Ghidra output](cases/en/llm4decompile-assembly-c-models.md) — The public research generates C from Linux x86_64 assembly or refines Ghidra pseudocode; rates from different models and input conditions are not a universal recovery score. **[Other AI-assisted work · Analysis test]**
- [A pre-buy contract check desk: Claude/Ghidra sell-path analysis and a corrected proxy flag](cases/en/claude-ghidra-contract-rug-check-desk.md) — A publisher describes bytecode-based buy gating and one corrected OpenZeppelin proxy false positive, without contract addresses, code or inspectable logs. **[Other AI-assisted work · Needs verification]**

## Contributing and selection

Submit new cases, additional verification, or corrections. Edit data/cases.json, run python3 scripts/build.py, and open a PR. See [Contributing (Chinese)](CONTRIBUTING.md).

Prefer original authors, concrete targets and recorded work. Merge multiple sources for one project. Distinguish author reports, static analysis and actual execution. X engagement is a dated snapshot, not proof of effectiveness.

## Scope and license

Unofficial collection. Results are attributed to authors and project records, not independently reproduced here. Only original summaries and public links are curated; no proprietary software, game assets, keys or private material are distributed.

Original summaries, curation data and code use the [MIT License](LICENSE). Linked content retains its own rights and licenses. Ensure you have the relevant authorization before investigating, reproducing or reusing it.

Organization inspired by [VoltAgent/awesome-openclaw-skills](https://github.com/VoltAgent/awesome-openclaw-skills). [Collection scope (Chinese)](research/collection-scope.md) · [Reference study (Chinese)](research/reference-structure.md)
